{"forms":[{"form":"reptiles","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A (typically scaly and egg-laying) cold-blooded vertebrate tetrapod animal, of the class Reptilia (excluding birds), such as a lizard, turtle, snake, crocodile, alligator, etc."]},{"glosses":["Any member of the class Reptilia, including birds."],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences","zoology"]},{"glosses":["Any creeping or crawling animal, one that moves along close to the ground on its belly or on short legs, such as a lizard, snake, salamander, frog, toad, or scorpion."],"tags":["dated"]},{"glosses":["An animal belonging to either the class Reptilia or the class Amphibia."],"synonyms":[{"word":"herptile"},{"word":"herp"}],"tags":["dated"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Prior to the 1990s, the non-mammalian synapsids were considered true reptiles.","type":"example"},{"text":"mammal-like reptile","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An ectothermic, scaly animal, whether sauropsid or synapsid, of the clade Amniota."],"tags":["dated","proscribed"]},{"glosses":["An abject, grovelling, loathsome or repulsive person."],"tags":["dated","figuratively"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɹɛp.taɪl/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈɹɛp.tɑj(ə)l/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈɹɛp.taɪl/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈɹɛp.taɪəl/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈɹep.tɑel/","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/ˈɹep.tɑe.əl/","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/ˈɾɛp.tʌɪl/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/ˈɾɛp.taɪl/","tags":["Wales"]}],"synonyms":[{"word":"creeper"},{"word":"reptile"}],"upstream_line":156861,"upstream_offset":721496556,"upstream_record_sha256":"55e006c2a15a275ade0daa478847bdc606b7c5e0451b1ef0c523c5cbe3d134c7","word":"reptile"}
